MossBlocks Guide

How to solve all 100 levels. Every solution here was replayed inside the game and works.

How to use this guide
  • Try the Hints first. Open the Full solution only if you're still stuck. Both stay closed so the next level isn't spoiled.
  • Arrows are your moves: ↑ up, ↓ down, ← left, → right. ←×3 means three steps left. "Push ↑×2" means walk into the crate twice, pushing it up two squares.
  • In the picture, press ▶| for the next step or ▶ Play to watch it all. Tap any step in the list to jump there.
  • Rows and columns are counted from the top-left outer wall, so the first floor row is row 1.
Tips that solve most levels
  • Never push a crate into a corner unless that corner is a pit. It can never come out.
  • A crate pushed against a wall can only slide along that wall. Make sure there's a pit along it.
  • Two crates side by side against a wall are stuck for good.
  • Work out which pit has to be filled last (usually the one deepest in a corner or corridor) and keep its path clear.
  • Before pushing, make sure you can still get around to the other side of the crate afterwards.
  • Undo has no limit. Use it instead of Reset.
